# Emoji: People & Body

[Emoji categories](emoji.md) · [Integration rules](../font-reference.md)

Browser glyphs are not Blit raster evidence. Fully-qualified/component entries form RGI; minimally-qualified/unqualified rows are compatibility variants, not additional RGI emoji.
